2026-10-25T10:10:40.0962019Z     def _is_language_support_installed(self, lang):

2026-10-25T10:10:40.0963558Z

2026-10-25T10:10:40.0969731Z         except OSError as e:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0970412Z             log.warning("Couldn't get supported locales: %s", e)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0971128Z             return True
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0971537Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0971952Z         match = find_best_locale_match(lang, output.splitlines())
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0972532Z

2026-10-25T10:10:40.0973745Z         return bool(match)

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1032744Z         # we can restore them when we have the file written out.

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1191310Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1191509Z         # to reflect updates from the compositor

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1192715Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1193057Z     def _on_properties_changed(self, interface, changed_props, invalid_props):
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1193931Z         if "X11Layout" in changed_props or "X11Variant" in changed_props:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1194900Z             layouts_variants = self._from_localed_format(changed_props["X11Layout"].get_string(),
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1195879Z                                                          changed_props["X11Variant"].get_string())
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1196741Z             # This part is a bit tricky. The signal processing here means that compositor has
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1197960Z             # changed current layouts configuration. This could happen for multiple reasons:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1198815Z             # - user changed the layout in compositor
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1199392Z             # - Anaconda set the layout to compositor
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1200027Z             # - any other magic logic for compositor (we just don't know)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1200633Z             #
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1201024Z             # The question is how we should behave:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1201754Z             # - we don't want to take compositor layouts to Anaconda because that will change
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1202572Z             #   what user will have in the installed system.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1203329Z             # - we don't want to force our layouts to compositor because that would forbid user
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1204387Z             #   to change compositor layout when Anaconda runs in background
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1204988Z             #
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1205586Z             # The best shot seems to just signal out that the layout has changed and nothing else.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1206170Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1206465Z             # layouts has changed in compositor, always emit this signal
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1207251Z             log.debug("Localed layouts has changed. Last known: '%s' current: '%s'",
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1208678Z                       self._last_layouts_variants, layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1209380Z             self.compositor_layouts_changed.emit(layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1209834Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1210055Z             # check if last selected variant has changed
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1210629Z             # nothing is selected in compositor
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1211149Z             if not layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1211672Z                 log.warning("Compositor layouts not set.")
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1212286Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit("")
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1212870Z             # we don't know last used layouts
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1213375Z             elif not self._last_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1213972Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different. "
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1214663Z                           "Missing information about last selected layouts.")
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1215423Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1216093Z             # selected (first) has changed
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1216685Z             elif layouts_variants[0] != self._last_layouts_variants[0]:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1217426Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different.")
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1218516Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1219055Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1219293Z             self._last_layouts_variants = layouts_variants

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1238862Z     def _set_layouts(self, layouts_variants, options=None, convert=False):
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1239531Z         """Set a new layouts for compositor.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1239864Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1240155Z         Extension of the parent code with compositor specific code.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1240739Z         """

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1242216Z             options = self.options

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1243502Z             )
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1243622Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1244224Z         # Disable keyboard layout switching by keyboard shortcut as it is hard to make this
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1245230Z         # working from the system to localed. The current layout is in general problematic to
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1245947Z         # decide.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1246297Z         log.debug(

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1250893Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1251088Z     def select_layout(self, layout_variant):
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1251668Z         """Select layout from the list of current layouts set.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1252074Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1252442Z         This will search for the given layout variant in the list and move it as first
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1253271Z         in the list. The first layout in systemd is taken as the used one.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1253729Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1254081Z         :param layout_variant: The layout to set, with format "layout (variant)"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1254749Z             (e.g. "cz (qwerty)")
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1255208Z         :type layout_variant: str
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1255743Z         :return: If the keyboard layout was activated
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1256288Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1256663Z         """
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1257163Z         # ignore compositor layouts but force Anaconda configuration
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1258349Z         layouts = self._user_layouts_variants
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1258718Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1258853Z         try:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1259257Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(layouts, layout_variant)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1259785Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1260275Z             return True
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1260676Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1261270Z             log.warning("Can't set layout: '%s' as first to the current set: %s",
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1262311Z                         layout_variant, layouts)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1262814Z             return False
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1263059Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1263222Z     @staticmethod
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1263663Z     def _shift_list(source_layouts, value_to_first):
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1264398Z         """Helper method to reorder list of layouts and move one as first in the list.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1264968Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1265353Z         We should preserve the ordering just shift items from start of the list to the
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1266085Z         end in the same order.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1266365Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1266586Z         When we want to set 2nd as first in this list:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1267103Z         ["cz", "es", "us"]
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1267507Z         The result should be:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1268358Z         ["es", "us", "cz"]
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1268634Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1268899Z         So the compositor has the same next layout as Anaconda.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1269341Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1269678Z         :raises: ValueError: if the list is small or the layout is not inside
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1270318Z         """
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1270725Z         value_id = source_layouts.index(value_to_first)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1271497Z         new_list = source_layouts[value_id:len(source_layouts)] + source_layouts[0:value_id]
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1272214Z         return new_list
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1272451Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1272617Z     def select_next_layout(self):
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1273144Z         """Select (make it first) next layout for compositor.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1273533Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1273940Z         Find current compositor layout in the list of defined layouts and set next to it as
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1274919Z         current (first) for compositor. We need to have user defined list because compositor
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1275968Z         layouts will change with the selection. Store this list when user is setting configuration
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1277647Z         to compositor. This list must not change ordering.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1278291Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1278623Z         :param user_layouts: List of layouts selected by user in Anaconda.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1279462Z         :type user_layouts: [str]
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1280001Z         :return: If switch was successful True otherwise False
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1280552Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1280903Z         """
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1281289Z         current_layout = self.current_layout_variant
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1281792Z         layout_id = 0
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1282014Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1282194Z         if not self._user_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1282927Z             log.error("Can't switch next layout - user defined keyboard layout is not present!")
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1283661Z             return False
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1283904Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1284050Z         # find next layout
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1284524Z         for i, v in enumerate(self._user_layouts_variants):
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1285079Z             if v == current_layout:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1285520Z                 layout_id = i + 1
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1286014Z                 layout_id %= len(self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1286402Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1286536Z         try:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1287007Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(self._user_layouts_variants,
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1287699Z                                            self._user_layouts_variants[layout_id])
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1288614Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1289077Z             return True
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1289459Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1290069Z             log.warning("Can't set next keyboard layout %s", self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1290746Z             return False

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1433602Z def get_missing_keyboard_configuration(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1433819Z     """Get keyboard configuration if not set by user.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1433825Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1433994Z     Algorithm works as this:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1434232Z     1. Check if full keyboard configuration is already set
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1434388Z        -> return these
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1434680Z     2. If no X layouts, check if they can be obtained from live system
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1434835Z        -> read X layouts
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1435031Z     3a. If still no configuration is present
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1435325Z        -> use DEFAULT_KEYBOARD as console layout and fall through to...
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1435645Z     3b. If only one of the keyboard layout or virtual console keymap is set
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1435834Z        -> convert one to the other by localed
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1435841Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1436153Z     :param localed_wrapper: instance of systemd-localed service wrapper
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1436340Z     :type localed_wrapper: LocaledWrapper
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1436571Z     :param x_layouts: list of X layout specifications
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1436730Z     :type x_layouts: list(str)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1436990Z     :param vc_keymap: virtual console keyboard mapping name
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1437144Z     :type vc_keymap: str
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1437389Z     :returns: tuple of X layouts and VC keyboard settings
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1437552Z     :rtype: (list(str), str))
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1437691Z     """
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1438366Z     if vc_keymap and x_layouts:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1438794Z         log.debug("Keyboard layouts and virtual console keymap already set - nothing to do")
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1439130Z         return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1439139Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1439349Z     live_keyboard = get_live_keyboard_instance()
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1439744Z     # layouts are not set by user, we should take a look for live configuration if available
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1439927Z     if not x_layouts and live_keyboard:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1440234Z         log.debug("Keyboard configuration from Live system is available")
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1440467Z         x_layouts = live_keyboard.read_keyboard_layouts()
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1440474Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1440648Z     if not vc_keymap and not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1441067Z         log.debug("Using default value %s for missing virtual console keymap", DEFAULT_KEYBOARD)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1441241Z         vc_keymap = DEFAULT_KEYBOARD
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1441247Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1441416Z     if not vc_keymap or not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1441878Z         x_layouts, vc_keymap = _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1441886Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1442050Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1442056Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1442061Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1442403Z def _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1442556Z     if not vc_keymap:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1442815Z         vc_keymap = localed_wrapper.convert_layouts(x_layouts)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1443197Z         log.debug("Missing virtual console keymap value %s converted from %s X layouts",
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1443363Z                   vc_keymap, x_layouts)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1443503Z     if not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1443752Z         x_layouts = localed_wrapper.convert_keymap(vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1444285Z         log.debug("Missing X layouts value %s converted from %s virtual console keymap",
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1444444Z                   x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1444450Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1444622Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1452792Z # Run a local bridge, web server, and browser for a particular Cockpit page.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1453130Z # This is useful for integration into .desktop files, for systems which don't
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1453477Z # have cockpit.socket enabled. The web server and browser run in an unshared
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1453805Z # network namespace, and thus are totally isolated from everything else.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1453941Z #
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1454078Z # Usage:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1454420Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 0
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1454761Z # For testing purposes one can also make cockpit-ws bind to all addresses
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1455021Z # (this is insecure and should not be used in prodcuction):
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1455360Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 1
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1455493Z #
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1455627Z set -eu
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1455634Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1455785Z THEME_ID="default"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1455930Z WEBUI_REMOTE=0
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1456085Z while getopts t:r: option
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1456218Z do
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1456358Z     case "${option}"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1456497Z         in
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1456658Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1456817Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1457216Z         *) echo "Usage: $0 [-t THEME_ID] [-r WEBUI_REMOTE]" >&2
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1457354Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1457490Z     esac
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1457626Z done
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1457632Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1458104Z URL_PATH="/cockpit/@localhost/anaconda-webui/index.html"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1458113Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1458383Z WEBUI_ADDRESS="127.0.0.1"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1458547Z if [[ "$WEBUI_REMOTE" == "1" ]]
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1458683Z then

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1459643Z # Function to setup user-related environment
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1459811Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1460107Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1460342Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1460479Z     #
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1460830Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1461015Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1461260Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1461463Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1461598Z     else
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1461957Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1462421Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1462428Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1462725Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1462904Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1463065Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1463212Z       fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1463344Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1463350Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1463549Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1464003Z     HOME_DIR=$(grep "^$INSTALLER_USER:" /etc/passwd | cut -d: -f6)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1464010Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1464271Z     # Check if the home directory exists, otherwise create it
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1464435Z     if [ ! -d "$HOME_DIR" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1464747Z       echo "The home directory $HOME_DIR does not exist. Creating it." >&2
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1464912Z       mkdir -p "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1465095Z       chown "$INSTALLER_USER" "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1465232Z     else
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1465476Z       echo "Home directory $HOME_DIR already exists." >&2
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1465617Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1465624Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1465859Z     # Return the user and home directory for further use
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1466029Z     echo "$INSTALLER_USER"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1466175Z }

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1466639Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1467188Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1467569Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1468094Z     timeout=60
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1468253Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1468260Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1468482Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1468679Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1468985Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1469131Z             exit 1
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1469253Z         fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1469364Z         sleep 1
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1469506Z         elapsed=$((elapsed + 1))
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1469614Z     done
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1469620Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1470303Z     # FIXME: Should be provided as an argument to this script instead of grepping the config file
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1470751Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(grep '^webui_web_engine =' /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf | cut -d'=' -f2)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1470936Z     if [ -n "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1471260Z         echo "Default browser set in anaconda.conf: $DEFAULT_BROWSER" >&2
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1471720Z         DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//')
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1471956Z         echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1472097Z     else
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1472418Z         echo "No default browser set in anaconda.conf, using firefox." >&2
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1472578Z         echo "firefox"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1472715Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1472856Z }

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1498529Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1505750Z fscheck() {
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1505904Z     local fstype="$1"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1506046Z     local fs="$2"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1506194Z     local d="${3:-$2}"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1506354Z     umount "$fs" >/dev/null 2>&1 || :
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1506502Z     case "$fstype" in
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1506642Z         ext[432])
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1506784Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1506969Z             flock "$d" e2fsck -yfv "$fs" || :
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1507099Z             ;;
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1507242Z         vfat|msdos)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1507391Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1507557Z             flock "$d" fsck.fat -avVw "$fs"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1507693Z             ;;
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1508341Z         btrfs)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1508576Z             printf '# btrfs check -p --repair --force'
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1508993Z             flock "$d" btrfs check -p --repair --force "$fs"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1509128Z             ;;
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1509265Z         xfs)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1509436Z             printf '\n# xfs_repair -v'
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1509618Z             flock "$d" xfs_repair -v "$fs"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1509757Z             ;;
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1509891Z         f2fs)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1510063Z             printf '# fsck.f2fs -afy'
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1510268Z             flock "$d" fsck.f2fs -afy "$fs"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1510406Z             ;;
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1510533Z         *)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1510849Z             printf "\n\tERROR:   Filesystem checking for '%s' is not\n \
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1511017Z                 available.\n" "$fstype"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1511148Z     esac
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1511303Z }

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1551150Z mountPersistentHome() {

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1551487Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1551866Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1552000Z   fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1552006Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1552261Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1552454Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1552711Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1552805Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1552911Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1553072Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1553295Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1553376Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1553488Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1553566Z   fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1553714Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1553718Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1553831Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1554065Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1554141Z     echo
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1554263Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1554495Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1554596Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1554670Z   fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1554846Z   if [ "$homedev" ] && [ "${cmdline##* rd.skipfsck[= ]}" = "$cmdline" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1555019Z     action "Checking home filesystem" fscheck "$homefs" "$homedev"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1555093Z   fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1555097Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1555197Z   # and finally do the mount
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1555333Z   mount ${mountopts:+$mountopts} "$homedev" /home
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1555498Z   # if we have /home under what's passed for persistent home, then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1555676Z   # we should make that the real /home.  useful for mtd device on olpc
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1555807Z   [ -d /home/home ] && mount --bind /home/home /home

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1556432Z   homedev=${cut%% *}

2026-10-25T10:10:40.0693382Z # This needs to be started only on X11 based systems
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0694081Z start_locale_sync_unit() {
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0700495Z     if [ -z "${WAYLAND_DISPLAY}" ] && [ -n "${DISPLAY}" ] && [ -e "/usr/lib/systemd/user/locale1-x11-sync.service" ]; then

2026-10-25T10:10:40.0702785Z

2026-10-25T10:10:40.0703713Z     if [ -z "${WAYLAND_DISPLAY}" ] && [ -n "${DISPLAY}" ] && [ -e "/usr/lib/systemd/user/locale1-x11-sync.service" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0704637Z         systemctl --user stop locale1-x11-sync.service
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0705150Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0705457Z }

2026-10-25T10:10:40.0705845Z # Detect and save the wayland socket before re-exec
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0706394Z if [ -n "$WAYLAND_DISPLAY" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0706873Z     if [ -e "$WAYLAND_DISPLAY_SOCKET" ]; then

2026-10-25T10:10:40.0708604Z     touch "${WAYLAND_DISPLAY_SOCKET}"

2026-10-25T10:10:40.0709870Z fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0710048Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0710259Z # Detect and save the a11y bus path before re-exec

2026-10-25T10:10:40.0711513Z     if [ -e "$ATSPI_BUSADDR" ]; then

2026-10-25T10:10:40.0712375Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0712750Z     _BUSCTL_OUTPUTREGEX='"([^"]+)"'
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0713473Z     _ATSPIADDR="$( busctl call --user org.a11y.Bus /org/a11y/bus org.a11y.Bus GetAddress )"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0714368Z     # Check that the busctl call did not fail and grab expected output
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0715037Z     # shellcheck disable=SC2181
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0715562Z     if [ "$?" -eq 0 ] && [[ $_ATSPIADDR =~ $_BUSCTL_OUTPUTREGEX ]]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0716167Z         touch "${ATSPI_BUSADDR}"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0716657Z         echo "${BASH_REMATCH[1]}" > ${ATSPI_BUSADDR}
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0717158Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0717468Z fi

2026-10-25T10:10:40.0717645Z

2026-10-25T10:10:40.0723043Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0723183Z     exit $ret

2026-10-25T10:10:40.0726166Z

2026-10-25T10:10:40.0726598Z       export "${variable?}"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0726832Z     done
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0727011Z fi

2026-10-25T10:10:40.0962015Z     def _is_language_support_installed(self, lang):
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0962785Z         """Is the support for the specified language installed?
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0963367Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0963995Z         The language is considered to be supported if we are not
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0964870Z         able to determine the supported locales due to missing tools.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0965494Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0965876Z         :param lang: a value for the LANG locale variable
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0966732Z         :return: False if the locale is known to be not supported, otherwise True
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0967545Z         """
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0968282Z         try:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0968943Z             output = execWithCapture("locale", ["-a"], root=self._sysroot)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0969727Z         except OSError as e:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.0970407Z             log.warning("Couldn't get supported locales: %s", e)

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1122165Z

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1188456Z

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1189466Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1190245Z         self.compositor_layouts_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1190897Z         self.compositor_selected_layout_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1191306Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1191506Z         # to reflect updates from the compositor
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1192240Z         self._localed_proxy.PropertiesChanged.connect(self._on_properties_changed)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1192711Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1193052Z     def _on_properties_changed(self, interface, changed_props, invalid_props):
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1193922Z         if "X11Layout" in changed_props or "X11Variant" in changed_props:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1194890Z             layouts_variants = self._from_localed_format(changed_props["X11Layout"].get_string(),
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1195870Z                                                          changed_props["X11Variant"].get_string())
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1196734Z             # This part is a bit tricky. The signal processing here means that compositor has
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1197718Z             # changed current layouts configuration. This could happen for multiple reasons:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1198807Z             # - user changed the layout in compositor
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1199384Z             # - Anaconda set the layout to compositor
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1200022Z             # - any other magic logic for compositor (we just don't know)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1200628Z             #
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1201019Z             # The question is how we should behave:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1201748Z             # - we don't want to take compositor layouts to Anaconda because that will change
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1202562Z             #   what user will have in the installed system.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1203323Z             # - we don't want to force our layouts to compositor because that would forbid user
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1204383Z             #   to change compositor layout when Anaconda runs in background
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1204984Z             #
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1205575Z             # The best shot seems to just signal out that the layout has changed and nothing else.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1206164Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1206452Z             # layouts has changed in compositor, always emit this signal
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1207247Z             log.debug("Localed layouts has changed. Last known: '%s' current: '%s'",
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1208671Z                       self._last_layouts_variants, layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1209373Z             self.compositor_layouts_changed.emit(layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1209828Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1210051Z             # check if last selected variant has changed
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1210621Z             # nothing is selected in compositor
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1211145Z             if not layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1211667Z                 log.warning("Compositor layouts not set.")
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1212283Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit("")
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1212860Z             # we don't know last used layouts
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1213372Z             elif not self._last_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1213969Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different. "
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1214658Z                           "Missing information about last selected layouts.")

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1216089Z             # selected (first) has changed
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1216682Z             elif layouts_variants[0] != self._last_layouts_variants[0]:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1217422Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different.")

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1219046Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1219289Z             self._last_layouts_variants = layouts_variants

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1252439Z         This will search for the given layout variant in the list and move it as first
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1253267Z         in the list. The first layout in systemd is taken as the used one.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1253726Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1254077Z         :param layout_variant: The layout to set, with format "layout (variant)"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1254741Z             (e.g. "cz (qwerty)")
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1255203Z         :type layout_variant: str
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1255724Z         :return: If the keyboard layout was activated
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1256282Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1256658Z         """
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1257158Z         # ignore compositor layouts but force Anaconda configuration
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1258341Z         layouts = self._user_layouts_variants
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1258714Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1258849Z         try:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1259248Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(layouts, layout_variant)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1259780Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1260267Z             return True
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1260672Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1261263Z             log.warning("Can't set layout: '%s' as first to the current set: %s",
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1262304Z                         layout_variant, layouts)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1262797Z             return False

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1263218Z     @staticmethod
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1263649Z     def _shift_list(source_layouts, value_to_first):
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1264390Z         """Helper method to reorder list of layouts and move one as first in the list.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1264962Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1265348Z         We should preserve the ordering just shift items from start of the list to the
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1266079Z         end in the same order.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1266362Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1266582Z         When we want to set 2nd as first in this list:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1267099Z         ["cz", "es", "us"]
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1267503Z         The result should be:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1268351Z         ["es", "us", "cz"]
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1268630Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1268895Z         So the compositor has the same next layout as Anaconda.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1269337Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1269674Z         :raises: ValueError: if the list is small or the layout is not inside
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1270313Z         """
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1270721Z         value_id = source_layouts.index(value_to_first)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1271486Z         new_list = source_layouts[value_id:len(source_layouts)] + source_layouts[0:value_id]
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1272209Z         return new_list
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1272447Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1272613Z     def select_next_layout(self):
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1273139Z         """Select (make it first) next layout for compositor.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1273530Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1273937Z         Find current compositor layout in the list of defined layouts and set next to it as
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1274914Z         current (first) for compositor. We need to have user defined list because compositor
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1275959Z         layouts will change with the selection. Store this list when user is setting configuration
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1277637Z         to compositor. This list must not change ordering.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1278283Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1278618Z         :param user_layouts: List of layouts selected by user in Anaconda.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1279261Z         :type user_layouts: [str]
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1279997Z         :return: If switch was successful True otherwise False
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1280548Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1280900Z         """
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1281285Z         current_layout = self.current_layout_variant
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1281789Z         layout_id = 0
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1282011Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1282191Z         if not self._user_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1282923Z             log.error("Can't switch next layout - user defined keyboard layout is not present!")
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1283658Z             return False
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1283901Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1284047Z         # find next layout
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1284520Z         for i, v in enumerate(self._user_layouts_variants):
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1285074Z             if v == current_layout:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1285516Z                 layout_id = i + 1
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1286010Z                 layout_id %= len(self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1286392Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1286532Z         try:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1287004Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(self._user_layouts_variants,
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1287689Z                                            self._user_layouts_variants[layout_id])
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1288608Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1289074Z             return True
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1289455Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1290065Z             log.warning("Can't set next keyboard layout %s", self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1290743Z             return False

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1433228Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1433599Z def get_missing_keyboard_configuration(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1433815Z     """Get keyboard configuration if not set by user.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1433822Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1433991Z     Algorithm works as this:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1434229Z     1. Check if full keyboard configuration is already set
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1434385Z        -> return these
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1434676Z     2. If no X layouts, check if they can be obtained from live system
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1434832Z        -> read X layouts
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1435028Z     3a. If still no configuration is present
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1435321Z        -> use DEFAULT_KEYBOARD as console layout and fall through to...
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1435637Z     3b. If only one of the keyboard layout or virtual console keymap is set
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1435831Z        -> convert one to the other by localed
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1435838Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1436150Z     :param localed_wrapper: instance of systemd-localed service wrapper
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1436337Z     :type localed_wrapper: LocaledWrapper
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1436568Z     :param x_layouts: list of X layout specifications
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1436727Z     :type x_layouts: list(str)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1436987Z     :param vc_keymap: virtual console keyboard mapping name
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1437141Z     :type vc_keymap: str
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1437386Z     :returns: tuple of X layouts and VC keyboard settings
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1437542Z     :rtype: (list(str), str))
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1437687Z     """
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1438359Z     if vc_keymap and x_layouts:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1438790Z         log.debug("Keyboard layouts and virtual console keymap already set - nothing to do")
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1438961Z         return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1439136Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1439346Z     live_keyboard = get_live_keyboard_instance()
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1439741Z     # layouts are not set by user, we should take a look for live configuration if available
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1439923Z     if not x_layouts and live_keyboard:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1440231Z         log.debug("Keyboard configuration from Live system is available")
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1440464Z         x_layouts = live_keyboard.read_keyboard_layouts()
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1440471Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1440644Z     if not vc_keymap and not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1441063Z         log.debug("Using default value %s for missing virtual console keymap", DEFAULT_KEYBOARD)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1441237Z         vc_keymap = DEFAULT_KEYBOARD
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1441244Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1441413Z     if not vc_keymap or not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1441870Z         x_layouts, vc_keymap = _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1441883Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1442047Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1442054Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1442059Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1442399Z def _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1442553Z     if not vc_keymap:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1442812Z         vc_keymap = localed_wrapper.convert_layouts(x_layouts)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1443194Z         log.debug("Missing virtual console keymap value %s converted from %s X layouts",
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1443360Z                   vc_keymap, x_layouts)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1443500Z     if not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1443748Z         x_layouts = localed_wrapper.convert_keymap(vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1444281Z         log.debug("Missing X layouts value %s converted from %s virtual console keymap",
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1444441Z                   x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1444448Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1444619Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1452789Z # Run a local bridge, web server, and browser for a particular Cockpit page.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1453127Z # This is useful for integration into .desktop files, for systems which don't
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1453474Z # have cockpit.socket enabled. The web server and browser run in an unshared
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1453802Z # network namespace, and thus are totally isolated from everything else.

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1454416Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 0

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1455927Z WEBUI_REMOTE=0

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1456215Z do
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1456355Z     case "${option}"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1456494Z         in
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1456654Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1456813Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1457213Z         *) echo "Usage: $0 [-t THEME_ID] [-r WEBUI_REMOTE]" >&2
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1457351Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1457487Z     esac
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1457614Z done

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1459640Z # Function to setup user-related environment
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1459807Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1460102Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1460337Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1460475Z     #
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1460825Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1461005Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1461257Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1461460Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1461595Z     else
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1461953Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1462417Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1462425Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1462721Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1462901Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1463062Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1463209Z       fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1463341Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1463348Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1463545Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1463824Z     HOME_DIR=$(grep "^$INSTALLER_USER:" /etc/passwd | cut -d: -f6)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1464008Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1464257Z     # Check if the home directory exists, otherwise create it
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1464431Z     if [ ! -d "$HOME_DIR" ]; then

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1466632Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1467180Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1467555Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1468087Z     timeout=60
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1468241Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1468256Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1468479Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1468676Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1468980Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1469128Z             exit 1
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1469250Z         fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1469362Z         sleep 1
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1469503Z         elapsed=$((elapsed + 1))
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1469611Z     done
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1469617Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1470293Z     # FIXME: Should be provided as an argument to this script instead of grepping the config file
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1470745Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(grep '^webui_web_engine =' /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf | cut -d'=' -f2)

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1471255Z         echo "Default browser set in anaconda.conf: $DEFAULT_BROWSER" >&2
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1471713Z         DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//')
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1471953Z         echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1472094Z     else
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1472414Z         echo "No default browser set in anaconda.conf, using firefox." >&2
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1472574Z         echo "firefox"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1472711Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1472852Z }

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1490549Z   # If not, create a dummy function that returns the input verbatim

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1491041Z

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1491470Z strstr() {

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1491747Z }
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1491753Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1491916Z # Run some action. Log its output.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1492061Z action() {
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1492205Z     local STRING rc
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1492212Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1492344Z     STRING=$1
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1492520Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1492650Z     shift
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1492789Z     if "$@"; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1492954Z         success "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1493100Z     else
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1493266Z         failure "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1493397Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1493539Z     rc=$?
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1493677Z     printf '\n'
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1493817Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1493954Z }
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1493967Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1494233Z # verbose ->> very (very!) old bootup look (prior to RHL-6.0?)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1494404Z # color ->> default bootup look
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1494690Z # other ->> default bootup look without ANSI colors or positioning
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1494836Z BOOTUP=color
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1495009Z # Column to start "[  OK  ]" label in:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1495141Z RES_COL=60
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1495333Z # terminal sequence to move to that column:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1495503Z MOVE_TO_COL="printf \\033[${RES_COL}G"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1495786Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'success' (bright green):
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1495963Z SETCOLOR_SUCCESS="printf \\033[1;32m"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1496389Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'failure' (bright red):
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1496567Z SETCOLOR_FAILURE="printf \\033[1;31m"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1496854Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'warning' (bright yellow):
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1497027Z SETCOLOR_WARNING="printf \\033[1;33m"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1497256Z # Terminal sequence to reset to the default color:
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1497422Z SETCOLOR_NORMAL="printf \\033[0;39m"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1497430Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1497599Z # NOTE: /dev/ttyS* is serial console.
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1498520Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1498785Z # where /dev/null is associated with standard input
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1498794Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1498955Z test_tty=$(LC_ALL=C tty)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1499097Z case "$test_tty" in
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1499252Z     /dev/ttyS* | 'not a tty')
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1499401Z         BOOTUP=serial
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1499545Z         MOVE_TO_COL=
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1499711Z         SETCOLOR_SUCCESS=
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1499859Z         SETCOLOR_FAILURE=
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1500011Z         SETCOLOR_WARNING=
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1500153Z         SETCOLOR_NORMAL=
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1500288Z         ;;
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1500423Z esac
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1500435Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1500596Z # Log that something succeeded

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1501213Z

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1502040Z     [ -x /bin/plymouth ] && /bin/plymouth --details
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1502182Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1502316Z }
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1502323Z

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1503491Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1503641Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1503778Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1503917Z     return 0
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1504057Z }
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1504064Z

2026-10-25T10:10:40.1551146Z mountPersistentHome() {
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1551295Z   # support label/uuid
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1551483Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1551662Z     [ "${homedev##UUID=}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1551863Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1551997Z   fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1552003Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1552257Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1552444Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1552704Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1552803Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1552909Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1553070Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1553293Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1553373Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1553486Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1553564Z   fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1553712Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1553717Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1553829Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1554063Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1554140Z     echo
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1554261Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1554489Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1554594Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1554668Z   fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1554844Z   if [ "$homedev" ] && [ "${cmdline##* rd.skipfsck[= ]}" = "$cmdline" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1555017Z     action "Checking home filesystem" fscheck "$homefs" "$homedev"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1555091Z   fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1555096Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1555195Z   # and finally do the mount
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1555323Z   mount ${mountopts:+$mountopts} "$homedev" /home
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1555496Z   # if we have /home under what's passed for persistent home, then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1555674Z   # we should make that the real /home.  useful for mtd device on olpc
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1555805Z   [ -d /home/home ] && mount --bind /home/home /home
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1555950Z   [ -x /sbin/restorecon ] && /sbin/restorecon /home
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1556059Z   [ -d /home/liveuser ] && USERADDARGS="-M"
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1556133Z }
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1556141Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1556250Z cut=${cmdline##* persistenthome[= ]}
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1556346Z if [ ${#cut} -ne ${#cmdline} ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1556430Z   homedev=${cut%% *}
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1556587Z elif [ -e /run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img ]; then
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1556710Z   homedev=/run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1556790Z fi
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1556794Z
2026-10-25T10:10:40.1556973Z # if we have a persistent /home, then we want to go ahead and mount it
